<0.9.0;\n3\t\n4\tinterface IERC20 {\n5\t    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n6\t}\n7\t\n8\t/**\n9\t * Routes USDC payouts straight to recipients and records them for the feed.\n10\t */\n11\tcontract Payouts {\n12\t    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n13\t\n14\t    event Paid(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 amount, string memo);\n15\t\n16\t    constructor(address _usdc) {\n17\t        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n18\t    }\n19\t\n20\t    function pay(address to, uint256 amount, string calldata memo) external {\n21\t        require(to != address(0), \"Payouts: zero recipient\");\n22\t        require(amount > 0, \"Payouts: zero amount\");\n23\t        require(usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, to, amount), \"Payouts: transfer failed\");\n24\t        emit Paid(msg.sender, to, amount, memo);\n25\t    }\n26\t}\n27\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c3931a04-18eb-4b4e-b035-9a275c621ebf","uuid":"0bfa10ff-052d-40a2-884d-e9a55ef6d283","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:41:27.336Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T154109Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/hardhat/contracts/Payouts.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\ninterface IERC20 {\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n}\n\n/**\n * Routes USDC payouts straight to recipients and records them for the feed.\n */\ncontract Payouts {\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    event Paid(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 amount, string memo);\n\n    constructor(address _usdc) {\n        usdc = IERC20(_usdc);\n    }\n\n    function pay(address to, uint256 amount, string calldata memo) external {\n        require(to != address(0), \"Payouts: zero recipient\");\n        require(amount > 0, \"Payouts: zero amount\");\n        require(usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, to, amount), \"Payouts: transfer failed\");\n        emit Paid(msg.sender, to, amount, memo);\n    }\n}\n","numLines":27,"startLine":1,"totalLines":27}}}

`${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n14\t\n15\tconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n16\t  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n17\t  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n18\t  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n19\t  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n20\t\n21\t  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n22\t\n23\t  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n24\t    contractName: \"USDC\",\n25\t    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n26\t    args: [connectedAddress],\n27\t  });\n28\t\n29\t  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n30\t    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n31\t    eventName: \"Paid\",\n32\t    fromBlock: 0n,\n33\t    watch: true,\n34\t  });\n35\t\n36\t  const handleApprove = async () => {\n37\t    try {\n38\t      await writeContractAsync({\n39\t        address: USDC.address,\n40\t        abi: USDC.abi,\n41\t        functionName: \"approve\",\n42\t        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n43\t      });\n44\t    } catch (e) {\n45\t      console.error(e);\n46\t    }\n47\t  };\n48\t\n49\t  const handlePay = async () => {\n50\t    try {\n51\t      await writeContractAsync({\n52\t        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n53\t        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n54\t        functionName: \"pay\",\n55\t        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n56\t      });\n57\t      setAmount(\"\");\n58\t      setMemo(\"\");\n59\t    } catch (e) {\n60\t      console.error(e);\n61\t    }\n62\t  };\n63\t\n64\t  return (\n65\t    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n66\t      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n67\t        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n68\t        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n69\t\n70\t        {!isConnected ? (optional)\"\n94\t              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n95\t              value={memo}\n96\t              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n97\t            />\n98\t\n99\t            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n100\t              <button\n101\t                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n102\t                disabled={isPending}\n103\t                onClick={handleApprove}\n104\t              >\n105\t                Approve\n106\t              </button>\n107\t              <button\n108\t                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n109\t                disabled={isPending}\n110\t                onClick={handlePay}\n111\t              >\n112\t                Send Payout\n113\t              </button>\n114\t            </div>\n115\t          </>\n116\t        )}\n117\t\n118\t        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n119\t        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n120\t          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n121\t            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n122\t              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n123\t              {\" paid \"}\n124\t              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n125\t              {\" \"}\n126\t              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n127\t              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n128\t            </div>\n129\t          ))}\n130\t          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n131\t        </div>\n132\t      </div>\n133\t    </div>\n134\t  );\n135\t};\n136\t\n137\texport default PayoutsPage;\n138\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c3931a04-18eb-4b4e-b035-9a275c621ebf","uuid":"7787ed7d-c0f5-40b6-8323-aedb2255d9be","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:41:38.279Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T154109Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/payouts/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { useScaffoldEventHistory, useScaffoldReadContract } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\nconst USDC = deployedContracts[8453].USDC;\n\nconst shorten = (addr?: string) => (addr ? `${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","numLines":138,"startLine":1,"totalLines":138}}}

{ useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { useInfiniteQuery, useQuery } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { Abi, AbiEvent, ExtractAbiEventNames } from \"abitype\";\nimport { BlockNumber, GetLogsParameters } from \"viem\";\nimport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { Config, UsePublicClientReturnType, useBlockNumber, usePublicClient } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { AllowedChainIds } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { replacer } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/common\";\nimport {\n  ContractAbi,\n  ContractName,\n  UseScaffoldEventHistoryConfig,\n  UseScaffoldEventHistoryData,\n} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\nconst getEvents = async (\n  getLogsParams: GetLogsParameters<AbiEvent | undefined, AbiEvent[] | undefined, boolean, BlockNumber, BlockNumber>,\n  publicClient?: UsePublicClientReturnType<Config, number>,\n  Options?: {\n    blockData?: boolean;\n    transactionData?: boolean;\n    receiptData?: boolean;\n  },\n) => {\n  const logs = await publicClient?.getLogs({\n    address: getLogsParams.address,\n    fromBlock: getLogsParams.fromBlock,\n    toBlock: getLogsParams.toBlock,\n    args: getLogsParams.args,\n    event: getLogsParams.event,\n  });\n  if (!logs) return undefined;\n\n  const finalEvents = await Promise.all(\n    logs.map(async log => {\n      return {\n        ...log,\n        blockData:\n          Options?.blockData && log.blockHash ? await publicClient?.getBlock({ blockHash: log.blockHash }) : null,\n        transactionData:\n          Options?.transactionData && log.transactionHash\n            ? await publicClient?.getTransaction({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n            : null,\n        receiptData:\n          Options?.receiptData && log.transactionHash\n            ? await publicClient?.getTransactionReceipt({ hash: log.transactionHash })\n            : null,\n      };\n    }),\n  );\n\n  return finalEvents;\n};\n\n/**\n * @deprecated **Recommended only for local (hardhat/anvil) chains and development.**\n * It uses getLogs which can overload RPC endpoints \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\nimport { useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n\n\nconst Home: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress } = useAccount();\n  const { address={connectedAddress} chain={targetNetwork} />\n          </div>\n          \n<p className=\"text-center text-lg\">\n  Get started by editing{\" \"}\n  <code className=\"italic bg-base-300 {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T154109Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/.claude/skills/qa\n\n# dApp QA — Pre-Ship Audit For Scaffold-ETH 2 Builds\n\n## What You Probably Got Wrong\n\n**\"The app deployed, so we are done.\"** For SE2 builds, shipping includes UX correctness, metadata, RPC reliability, contract verification, and branding cleanup.\n\n**\"The flow is obvious.\"** If Connect, Network, Approve, and Action are not strictly one-at-a-time with proper pending states, users will make duplicate or failing transactions.\n\n**\"SE2 defaults are fine in production.\"** Default README/footer/title/favicon and default RPC fallbacks are template scaffolding, not production decisions.\n\n**\"Pass means no console errors.\"** QA pass/fail here is behavioral and user-facing: real wallet flow, mobile deep-link behavior, readable errors, and trust signals must be validated.\n\nGive this to a fresh agent after the dApp is built. The reviewer should:\n\n1. Read the source code (`app/`, `components/`, `contracts/`)\n2. Open the app in a browser and click through every flow\n3. Check every item below — report PASS/FAIL, don't fix\n\n---\n\n## 🚨 Critical: Wallet Flow — Button Not Text\n\nOpen the app with NO wallet connected.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Text saying \"Connect your wallet to play\" / \"Please connect to continue\" / any paragraph telling the user to connect\n- ✅ **PASS:** A big, obvious Connect Wallet **button** is the primary UI element\n\n**This is the most common AI agent mistake.** Every stock LLM writes a `<p>Please connect your wallet</p>` instead of rendering `<RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />`.\n\n---\n\n## 🚨 Critical: Four-State Button Flow\n\nThe app must show exactly ONE primary button at a time, progressing through:\n\n```\n1. Not connected  → Connect Wallet button\n2. Wrong network  → Switch to [Chain] button\n3. Needs approval → Approve button\n4. Ready          → Action button (Stake/Deposit/Swap)\n```\n\nCheck specifically:\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Approve and Action buttons both visible simultaneously\n- ❌ **FAIL:** No network check — app tries to work on wrong chain and fails silently\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Main onchain CTA renders instead of a \"Switch to [Chain]\" button when the connected wallet is on the wrong network. SE-2's header `WrongNetworkDropdown` is **not sufficient** — the action button itself must become the switch CTA, or the user clicks Sign/Stake/Deposit on the wrong chain and eats a silent wagmi error.\n- ❌ **FAIL:** User can click Approve, sign in wallet, come back, and click Approve again while tx is pending\n- ✅ **PASS:** One button at a time. Approve button shows spinner, stays disabled until block confirms onchain. Then switches to the action button.\n- ✅ **PASS:** Action button's render path branches on `useChainId() === targetNetwork.id` (or equivalent); mismatch renders a `useSwitchChain`-driven \"Switch to [Chain]\" button in the **same slot** as the primary CTA.\n\n**In the code:** the button's `disabled` prop must be tied to `isPending` from `useScaffoldWriteContract`. Verify it uses `useScaffoldWriteContract` (waits for block confirmation), NOT raw wagmi `useWriteContract` (resolves on wallet signature):\n\n```\ngrep -rn \"useWriteContract\" packages/nextjs/\n```\nAny match outside scaffold-eth internals → bug.\n\n**Watch out: two gaps, both allow double-approve.**\n\n`isPending` from wagmi drops to `false` when the wallet returns the tx hash — not when the tx confirms. `writeContractAsync` is still awaiting confirmation. During that window `isPending = false` AND `approveCooldown = false` → button re-enables mid-flight.\n\nFix requires TWO states:\n- `approvalSubmitting` — set at top of handler, cleared in `finally {}` (covers click→hash gap)\n- `approveCooldown` — set after `await` resolves, cleared after 4s + refetch (covers confirm→cache gap)\n\n```tsx\nconst [approvalSubmitting, setApprovalSubmitting] = useState(false);\nconst [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n\nconst handleApprove = async () => {\n  if (approvalSubmitting || approveCooldown) return;\n  setApprovalSubmitting(true);\n  try {\n    await approveWrite({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [spender, amount] });\n    setApproveCooldown(true);\n    setTimeout(() => { setApproveCooldown(false); refetchAllowance(); }, 4000);\n  } catch (e) {\n    notifyError(\"Approval failed\");\n  } finally {\n    setApprovalSubmitting(false); // must be finally — releases on rejection too\n  }\n};\n\n<button disabled={isPending || approvalSubmitting || approveCooldown}>\n```\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Button `disabled` only reads `isPending` or only `approveCooldown`\n- ❌ **FAIL:** No `approvalSubmitting` state, or it's not cleared in `finally {}`\n- ✅ **PASS:** `disabled={isPending || approvalSubmitting || approveCooldown}` with both states managed correctly\n\n---\n\n## 🚨 Critical: SE2 Branding Removal\n\nAI agents treat the scaffold as sacred and leave all default branding in place.\n\n- [ ] **Footer:** Remove BuidlGuidl links, \"Built with 🏗️ SE2\", \"Fork me\" link, support links. Replace with project's own repo link or clean it out\n- [ ] **Tab title:** Must be the app name, NOT \"Scaffold-ETH 2\" or \"SE-2 App\" or \"App Name | Scaffold-ETH 2\"\n- [ ] **README:** Must describe THIS project. Not the SE2 template README. Remove \"Built with Scaffold-ETH 2\" sections and SE2 doc links\n- [ ] **Favicon:** Must not be the SE2 default\n\n---\n\n## Important: Contract Address Display\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** The deployed contract address appears nowhere on the page\n- ✅ **PASS:** Contract address displayed using `<Address/>` component (blockie, ENS, copy, explorer link)\n\nAgents display the connected wallet address but forget to show the contract the user is interacting with.\n\n---\n\n## Important: Address Input — Always `<AddressInput/>`\n\n**EVERY input that accepts an Ethereum address must use `<AddressInput/>`, not a plain `<input type=\"text\">`.**\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `<input type=\"text\" placeholder=\"0x...\" value={addr} onChange={e => setAddr(e.target.value)} />`\n- ✅ **PASS:** `<AddressInput value={addr} onChange={setAddr} placeholder=\"0x... or ENS name\" />`\n\n`<AddressInput/>` gives you ENS resolution (type \"vitalik.eth\" → resolves to address), blockie avatar preview, validation, and paste handling. A raw text input is unacceptable for address collection.\n\n**In SE2, it's in `@scaffold-ui/components`:**\n```typescript\nimport { AddressInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\n// or\nimport { AddressInput } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\"; // if re-exported\n```\n\n**Quick check:**\n```bash\ngrep -rn 'type=\"text\"' packages/nextjs/app/ | grep -i \"addr\\|owner\\|recip\\|0x\"\ngrep -rn 'placeholder=\"0x' packages/nextjs/app/\n```\nAny match → **FAIL**. Replace with `<AddressInput/>`.\n\nThe pair: `<Address/>` for **display**, `<AddressInput/>` for **input**. Always.\n\n---\n\n## Important: USD Values\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Token amounts shown as \"1,000 TOKEN\" or \"0.5 ETH\" with no dollar value\n- ✅ **PASS:** \"0.5 ETH (~$1,250)\" with USD conversion\n\nAgents never add USD values unprompted. Check every place a token or ETH amount is displayed, including inputs.\n\n---\n\n## Important: OG Image Must Be Absolute URL\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `images: [\"/thumbnail.jpg\"]` — relative path, breaks unfurling everywhere\n- ✅ **PASS:** `images: [\"https://yourdomain.com/thumbnail.jpg\"]` — absolute production URL\n\nQuick check:\n```\ngrep -n \"og:image\\|images:\" packages/nextjs/app/layout.tsx\n```\n\n---\n\n## Important: RPC & Polling Config\n\nOpen `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`:\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `pollingInterval: 30000` (default — makes the UI feel broken, 30 second update lag)\n- ✅ **PASS:** `pollingInterval: 3000`\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Using default Alchemy API key that ships with SE2\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Code references `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_*` but the variable isn't actually set in the deployment environment (Vercel/hosting). Falls back to public RPC like `mainnet.base.org` which is rate-limited\n- ✅ **PASS:** `rpcOverrides` uses `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_*` variables AND the env var is confirmed set on the hosting platform\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `services/web3/wagmiConfig.tsx` still includes bare `http()` fallback transport (silently hits public RPCs in parallel, causing rate limits)\n- ✅ **PASS:** Bare `http()` fallback removed; only intended configured transports remain\n\n**Verify the env var is set, not just referenced.** AI agents will change the code to use `process.env`, see the pattern matches PASS, and move on — without ever setting the actual variable on Vercel/hosting. Check:\n```bash\nvercel env ls | grep RPC\n```\n\n---\n\n## Important: SE2 `externalContracts.ts` Registration\n\nScaffold hooks only work with contracts registered in `deployedContracts.ts` (auto-generated) or `externalContracts.ts` (manual). If external contracts are not registered, frontend reads/writes silently fail.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Frontend code references token/protocol contracts that are missing from `packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts`\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `deployedContracts.ts` manually edited to add external contracts\n- ✅ **PASS:** All external contracts are defined in `externalContracts.ts` with correct chain, address, and ABI\n\nExample:\n```typescript\nexport default {\n  8453: {\n    USDC: {\n      address: \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\",\n      abi: [...],\n    },\n  },\n} as const;\n```\n\nNever edit `deployedContracts.ts` directly. It is regenerated on deploy.\n\n---\n\n## Important: Dark Mode — No Hardcoded Dark Backgrounds\n\nAI agents love the aesthetic of a dark UI and will hardcode it directly on the page wrapper:\n\n```tsx\n// ❌ FAIL — hardcoded black background, ignores system preference AND DaisyUI theme\n<div className=\"min-h-screen bg-[#0a0a0a] text-white\">\n```\n\nThis bypasses the entire DaisyUI theme system. Light-mode users get a black page. The `SwitchTheme` toggle in the SE2 header stops working. `prefers-color-scheme` is ignored.\n\n**Check for this pattern:**\n```bash\ngrep -rn 'bg-\\[#0\\|bg-black\\|bg-gray-9\\|bg-zinc-9\\|bg-neutral-9\\|bg-slate-9' packages/nextjs/app/\n```\nAny match on a root layout div or page wrapper → **FAIL**.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Root page wrapper uses a hardcoded hex color or Tailwind dark bg class (`bg-[#0a0a0a]`, `bg-black`, `bg-zinc-900`, etc.)\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `SwitchTheme` toggle is present in the header but the page ignores `data-theme` entirely\n- ✅ **PASS:** All backgrounds use DaisyUI semantic variables — `bg-base-100`, `bg-base-200`, `text-base-content`\n- ✅ **PASS (dark-only exception):** Theme is explicitly forced via `data-theme=\"dark\"` on `<html>` **AND** the `<SwitchTheme/>` component is removed from the header\n\n**The fix:**\n```tsx\n// ✅ CORRECT — responds to light/dark toggle and prefers-color-scheme\n<div className=\"min-h-screen bg-base-200 text-base-content\">\n```\n\n---\n\n## Important: Phantom Wallet in RainbowKit\n\nPhantom is NOT in the SE2 default wallet list. A lot of users have Phantom — if it's missing, they can't connect.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Phantom wallet not in the RainbowKit wallet list\n- ✅ **PASS:** `phantomWallet` is in `wagmiConnectors.tsx`\n\n---\n\n## Important: Mobile Deep Linking\n\n**RainbowKit v2 / WalletConnect v2 does NOT auto-deep-link to the wallet app.** It relies on push notifications instead, which are slow and unreliable. You must implement deep linking yourself.\n\nOn mobile, when a user taps a button that needs a signature, it must open their wallet app. Test this: open the app on a phone, connect a wallet via WalletConnect, tap an action button — does the wallet app open with the transaction ready to sign?\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Nothing happens, user has to manually switch to their wallet app\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Deep link fires BEFORE the transaction — user arrives at wallet with nothing to sign\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `window.location.href = \"rainbow://\"` called before `writeContractAsync()` — navigates away and the TX never fires\n- ❌ **FAIL:** It opens the wrong wallet (e.g. opens MetaMask when user connected with Rainbow)\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Deep links inside a wallet's in-app browser (unnecessary — you're already in the wallet)\n- ✅ **PASS:** Every transaction button fires the TX first, then deep links to the correct wallet app after a delay\n\n### How to implement it\n\n**Pattern: `writeAndOpen` helper.** Fire the write call first (sends the TX request over WalletConnect), then deep link after a delay to switch the user to their wallet:\n\n```typescript\nconst writeAndOpen = useCallback(\n  <T,>(writeFn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {\n    const promise = writeFn(); // Fire TX — does gas estimation + WC relay\n    setTimeout(openWallet, 2000); // Switch to wallet AFTER request is relayed\n    return promise;\n  },\n  [openWallet],\n);\n\n// Usage — wraps every write call:\nawait writeAndOpen(() => gameWrite({ functionName: \"click\", args: [...] }));\n```\n\n**Why 2 seconds?** `writeContractAsync` must estimate gas, encode calldata, and relay the signing request through WalletConnect's servers. 300ms is too fast — the wallet won't have received the request yet.\n\n**Detecting the wallet:** `connector.id` from wagmi says `\"walletConnect\"`, NOT `\"rainbow\"` or `\"metamask\"`. You must check multiple sources:\n\n```typescript\nconst openWallet = useCallback(() => {\n  if (typeof window === \"undefined\") return;\n  const isMobile = /iPhone|iPad|iPod|Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent);\n  if (!isMobile || window.ethereum) return; // Skip if desktop or in-app browser\n\n  // Check connector, wagmi storage, AND WalletConnect session data\n  const allIds = [connector?.id, connector?.name,\n    localStorage.getItem(\"wagmi.recentConnectorId\")]\n    .filter(Boolean).join(\" \").toLowerCase();\n\n  let wcWallet = \"\";\n  try {\n    const wcKey = Object.keys(localStorage).find(k => k.startsWith(\"wc@2:client\"));\n    if (wcKey) wcWallet = (localStorage.getItem(wcKey) || \"\").toLowerCase();\n  } catch {}\n  const search = `${allIds} ${wcWallet}`;\n\n  const schemes: [string[], string][] = [\n    [[\"rainbow\"], \"rainbow://\"],\n    [[\"metamask\"], \"metamask://\"],\n    [[\"coinbase\", \"cbwallet\"], \"cbwallet://\"],\n    [[\"trust\"], \"trust://\"],\n    [[\"phantom\"], \"phantom://\"],\n  ];\n\n  for (const [keywords, scheme] of schemes) {\n    if (keywords.some(k => search.includes(k))) {\n      window.location.href = scheme;\n      return;\n    }\n  }\n}, [connector]);\n```\n\n**Key rules:**\n1. **Fire TX first, deep link second.** Never `window.location.href` before the write call\n2. **Skip deep link if `window.ethereum` exists** — means you're already in the wallet's in-app browser\n3. **Check WalletConnect session data** in localStorage — `connector.id` alone won't tell you which wallet\n4. **Use simple scheme URLs** like `rainbow://` — not `rainbow://dapp/...` which reloads the page\n5. **Wrap EVERY write call** — approve, action, claim, batch — not just the main one\n\n---\n\n## 🚨 Critical: Contract Verification on Block Explorer\n\nAfter deploying, every contract MUST be verified on the block explorer. Unverified contracts are a trust red flag — users can't read the source code, and it looks like you're hiding something.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** Block explorer shows \"Contract source code not verified\" for any deployed contract\n- ✅ **PASS:** All deployed contracts show verified source code with a green checkmark on the block explorer\n\n**How to check:** Take each contract address from `deployedContracts.ts`, open it on the block explorer (Etherscan, Basescan, Arbiscan, etc.), and look for the \"Contract\" tab with a ✅ checkmark. If it shows bytecode only — not verified.\n\n**How to fix (SE2):**\n```bash\nyarn verify --network mainnet   # or base, arbitrum, optimism, etc.\n```\n\n**How to fix (Foundry):**\n```bash\nforge verify-contract <ADDRESS> <CONTRACT> --chain <CHAIN_ID> --etherscan-api-key $ETHERSCAN_API_KEY\n```\n\nAI agents frequently skip verification because `yarn deploy` succeeds and they move on. Deployment is not done until verification passes.\n\n---\n\n## Important: Button Loading State — DaisyUI `loading` Class Is Wrong\n\nAI agents almost always implement button loading states incorrectly when using DaisyUI + SE2.\n\n**The mistake:** Adding `loading` as a class directly on a `btn`:\n\n```tsx\n// ❌ FAIL — DaisyUI's `loading` class on a `btn` replaces the entire button content\n// with a spinner that fills the full button. No text, misaligned, looks broken.\n<button className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}>\n  {isPending ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n</button>\n```\n\n**The fix:** Remove `loading` from the button class, add an inline `loading-spinner` span inside the button alongside the text:\n\n```tsx\n// ✅ PASS — small spinner inside the button, text visible next to it\n<button className=\"btn btn-primary\" disabled={isPending}>\n  {isPending && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n  {isPending ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n</button>\n```\n\n**Check for this in code:**\n```bash\ngrep -rn '\"loading\"' packages/nextjs/app/\n```\nAny `\"loading\"` string in a button's className → **FAIL**.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `className={... isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}` on a button\n- ✅ **PASS:** `<span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" />` inside the button\n\n---\n\n## Important: SE2 Pill-Shaped Inputs (`--radius-field`)\n\nSE2 DaisyUI theme defaults to `--radius-field: 9999rem`, which creates pill-shaped textareas/selects and often clips content.\n\n- ❌ **FAIL:** `--radius-field: 9999rem` remains in `packages/nextjs/styles/globals.css`\n- ✅ **PASS:** `--radius-field` is changed to `0.5rem` (or similar) in both light and dark theme blocks\n\nFix in theme (not per component):\n```css\n/* In BOTH @plugin \"daisyui/theme\" blocks */\n--radius-field: 0.5rem;\n```\n\nDo not patch this by sprinkling `rounded-*` utility classes per input; fix it once at theme level.\n\n---\n\n## SE2 References\n\n- Docs: https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/\n- UI Components: https://ui.scaffoldeth.io/\n- SpeedRun Ethereum: https://speedrunethereum.com/\n\n---\n\n## Audit Summary\n\nReport each as PASS or FAIL:\n\n### Ship-Blocking\n- [ ] Wallet connection shows a BUTTON, not text\n- [ ] Wrong network shows a Switch button **in the primary CTA slot** (not only in the header dropdown)\n- [ ] One button at a time (Connect → Network → Approve → Action)\n- [ ] Approve button locked through full cycle: `approvalSubmitting` (click→hash), `approveCooldown` (confirm→cache refresh) — both states required, both on the It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Hardhat, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum configOrName, writeContractParams, chainId: undefined }\n      : (configOrName as UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>);\n  const { contractName, chainId, writeContractParams: finalWriteContractParams } = finalConfig;\n\n  const wagmiConfig = useConfig();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n      console.warn(\n        \"Using `useScaffoldWriteContract` with a string parameter is deprecated. config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n * @param writeContractParams - wagmi's useWriteContract parameters\n */\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  configOrName: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName> | TContractName,\n  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName> {\n  const finalConfig =\n    typeof configOrName === \"string\"\n      ? { contractName: configOrName, writeContractParams, chainId: undefined }\n      : (configOrName as UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>);\n  const { contractName, chainId, writeContractParams: finalWriteContractParams } = finalConfig;\n\n  const wagmiConfig = useConfig();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n      console.warn(\n        \"Using `useScaffoldWriteContract` with a string parameter is deprecated. Please use the object parameter version instead.\",\n      );\n    }\n  }, [configOrName]);\n\n  const { chain: accountChain } = useAccount();\n  const writeTx = useTransactor();\n  const [isMining, setIsMining] = useState(false);\n\n  const wagmiContractWrite = useWriteContract(finalWriteContractParams);\n\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n=== hooks index ===\nexport * from \"./useContractLogs\";\nexport * from \"./useCopyToClipboard\";\nexport * from \"./useDeployedContractInfo\";\nexport * from \"./useFetchBlocks\";\nexport * from \"./useNetworkColor\";\nexport * from \"./useOutsideClick\";\nexport * from \"./useScaffoldContract\";\nexport * from \"./useScaffoldEventHistory\";\nexport * from \"./useScaffoldReadContract\";\nexport * from \"./useScaffoldWatchContractEvent\";\nexport * from \"./useScaffoldWriteContract\";\nexport * from \"./useTargetNetwork\";\nexport * from \"./useTransactor\";\nexport * from \"./useSelectedNetwork\";","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { Address, AddressInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { RainbowKitCustomConnectButton } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\nimport {\n  useScaffoldEventHistory,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\nconst DEPLOY_BLOCK = BigInt(PAYOUTS.deployedOnBlock);\n\nconst parseAmount = (value: string) => {\n  try {\n    return value ? parseUnits(value, 6) : 0n;\n  } catch {\n    return 0n;\n  }\n};\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: DEPLOY_BLOCK,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const onCorrectNetwork = chainId === targetNetwork.id;\n  const parsedAmount = parseAmount(amount);\n  const needsApproval = allowance !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n && allowance < parsedAmount;\n  const busy = isApproving || isPaying;\n\n  const validate = () => {\n    if (!isAddress(recipient)) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address\");\n      return false;\n    }\n    if (parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter an amount greater than 0\");\n      return false;\n    }\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Amount exceeds your USDC balance\");\n      return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n  };\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (busy || !validate()) return;\n    await approveAsync({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS.address, parsedAmount] });\n    await refetchAllowance();\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (busy || !validate()) return;\n    await payAsync({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n    await refetchAllowance();\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  const latestPayouts = [...(payouts ?? [])].reverse();\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-base-200 text-base-content\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-4\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        <div className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6 flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <span>Payouts contract:</span>\n          <Address address={PAYOUTS.address} size=\"xs\" />\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC</p>\n\n            <AddressInput value={recipient} onChange={setRecipient} placeholder=\"0x... or ENS name\" />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full my-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            {!onCorrectNetwork ? (\n              <button\n                className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n                disabled={isSwitching}\n                onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n              >\n                {isSwitching && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n                Switch to {targetNetwork.name}\n              </button>\n            ) : needsApproval ? (\n              <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handleApprove}>\n                {isApproving && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n                {isApproving ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve USDC\"}\n              </button>\n            ) : (\n              <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handlePay}>\n                {isPaying && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n                {isPaying ? \"Sending...\" : \"Send Payout\"}\n              </button>\n            )}\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {latestPayouts.map(payout => (\n            <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${payout.logIndex}`} className=\"border border-base-300 rounded p-3\">\n              <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1\">\n                <Address address={payout.args.from} size=\"xs\" />\n                <span className=\"opacity-70\">paid</span>\n                <Address address={payout.args.to} size=\"xs\" />\n                <span className=\"font-medium\">\n                  {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n                </span>\n              </div>\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {latestPayouts.length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3910,"cache_read_input_tokens":57697,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3910},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c3931a04-18eb-4b4e-b035-9a275c621ebf","uuid":"042914ed-6513-49f4-8dc0-fa026595765a","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:44:20.453Z","request_id":"req_011CdM93TH3YdfgF4yGK3YGE"}

`${addr.slice(0, 6)}...${addr.slice(-4)}` : \"\");\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { writeContractAsync, isPending } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: 0n,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC.address,\n        abi: USDC.abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [PAYOUTS.address, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6)],\n      });\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    try {\n      await writeContractAsync({\n        address: PAYOUTS.address,\n        abi: PAYOUTS.abi,\n        functionName: \"pay\",\n        args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parseUnits(amount || \"0\", 6), memo],\n      });\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      setMemo(\"\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error(e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-black text-white\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <p className=\"mt-4\">Please connect your wallet to send a payout.</p>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">\n              Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n            </p>\n\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              placeholder=\"0x... recipient address\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={recipient}\n              onChange={e => setRecipient(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full mb-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-primary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handleApprove}\n              >\n                Approve\n              </button>\n              <button\n                className={`btn btn-secondary ${isPending ? \"loading\" : \"\"}`}\n                disabled={isPending}\n                onClick={handlePay}\n              >\n                Send Payout\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {(payouts ?? []).map((payout, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"border border-gray-700 rounded p-3\">\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.from)}</span>\n              {\" paid \"}\n              <span className=\"font-mono\">{shorten(payout.args.to)}</span>\n              {\" \"}\n              {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {(payouts ?? []).length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","userModified":false}}

});","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { formatUnits, isAddress, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { Address, AddressInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport deployedContracts from \"~~/contracts/deployedContracts\";\nimport { RainbowKitCustomConnectButton } from \"~~/components/scaffold-eth\";\nimport {\n  useScaffoldEventHistory,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst PAYOUTS = deployedContracts[8453].Payouts;\nconst DEPLOY_BLOCK = BigInt(PAYOUTS.deployedOnBlock);\n\nconst parseAmount = (value: string) => {\n  try {\n    return value ? parseUnits(value, 6) : 0n;\n  } catch {\n    return 0n;\n  }\n};\n\nconst PayoutsPage: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address: connectedAddress, isConnected, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [memo, setMemo] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const { data: usdcBalance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [connectedAddress],\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [connectedAddress, PAYOUTS.address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: approveAsync, isMining: isApproving } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n  });\n  const { writeContractAsync: payAsync, isMining: isPaying } = useScaffoldWriteContract({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n  });\n\n  const { data: payouts } = useScaffoldEventHistory({\n    contractName: \"Payouts\",\n    eventName: \"Paid\",\n    fromBlock: DEPLOY_BLOCK,\n    watch: true,\n  });\n\n  const onCorrectNetwork = chainId === targetNetwork.id;\n  const parsedAmount = parseAmount(amount);\n  const needsApproval = allowance !== undefined && parsedAmount > 0n && allowance < parsedAmount;\n  const busy = isApproving || isPaying;\n\n  const validate = () => {\n    if (!isAddress(recipient)) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter a valid recipient address\");\n      return false;\n    }\n    if (parsedAmount <= 0n) {\n      notification.error(\"Enter an amount greater than 0\");\n      return false;\n    }\n    if (usdcBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount > usdcBalance) {\n      notification.error(\"Amount exceeds your USDC balance\");\n      return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n  };\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (busy || !validate()) return;\n    await approveAsync({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [PAYOUTS.address, parsedAmount] });\n    await refetchAllowance();\n  };\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    if (busy || !validate()) return;\n    await payAsync({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });\n    await refetchAllowance();\n    setAmount(\"\");\n    setMemo(\"\");\n  };\n\n  const latestPayouts = [...(payouts ?? [])].reverse();\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-base-200 text-base-content\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-xl mx-auto pt-10 px-4 pb-16\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-bold mb-1\">USDC Payouts</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-4\">Send a USDC payout to any teammate or contributor on Base.</p>\n\n        <div className=\"text-sm opacity-70 mb-6 flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <span>Payouts contract:</span>\n          <Address address={PAYOUTS.address} size=\"xs\" />\n        </div>\n\n        {!isConnected ? (\n          <RainbowKitCustomConnectButton />\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            <p className=\"mb-4\">Your balance: {usdcBalance !== undefined ? formatUnits(usdcBalance, 6) : \"0\"} USDC</p>\n\n            <AddressInput value={recipient} onChange={setRecipient} placeholder=\"0x... or ENS name\" />\n            <input\n              type=\"number\"\n              placeholder=\"Amount (USDC)\"\n              className=\"input input-bordered w-full my-3\"\n              value={amount}\n              onChange={e => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            />\n            <textarea\n              placeholder=\"What is this payout for? (optional)\"\n              className=\"textarea textarea-bordered w-full mb-4\"\n              value={memo}\n              onChange={e => setMemo(e.target.value)}\n            />\n\n            {!onCorrectNetwork ? (\n              <button\n                className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n                disabled={isSwitching}\n                onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n              >\n                {isSwitching && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n                Switch to {targetNetwork.name}\n              </button>\n            ) : needsApproval ? (\n              <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handleApprove}>\n                {isApproving && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n                {isApproving ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve USDC\"}\n              </button>\n            ) : (\n              <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handlePay}>\n                {isPaying && <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm mr-2\" />}\n                {isPaying ? \"Sending...\" : \"Send Payout\"}\n              </button>\n            )}\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <h2 className=\"text-xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-3\">Latest payouts</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n          {latestPayouts.map(payout => (\n            <div key={`${payout.transactionHash}-${payout.logIndex}`} className=\"border border-base-300 rounded p-3\">\n              <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1\">\n                <Address address={payout.args.from} size=\"xs\" />\n                <span className=\"opacity-70\">paid</span>\n                <Address address={payout.args.to} size=\"xs\" />\n                <span className=\"font-medium\">\n                  {payout.args.amount !== undefined ? formatUnits(payout.args.amount, 6) : \"0\"} USDC\n                </span>\n              </div>\n              {payout.args.memo ? <span className=\"block opacity-70 mt-1\">“{payout.args.memo}”</span> : null}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {latestPayouts.length === 0 && <p className=\"opacity-60\">No payouts yet.</p>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PayoutsPage;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":90,"oldLines":7,"newStart":90,"newLines":7,"lines":[" ","   const handlePay = async () => {","     if (busy || !validate()) return;","-    await payAsync({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient, parsedAmount, memo] });","+    await payAsync({ functionName: \"pay\", args: [recipient as `0x${string}`, parsedAmount, memo] });","     await refetchAllowance();","     setAmount(\"\");","     setMemo(\"\");"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdM9Bgr6V3NP5L9LtGti2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015ECagx933xFYjYZAzefxgu","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/qa-goal-002/2026-07-24T154109Z-claude-with-skill-2/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Payouts\n\nA dApp for sending USDC payouts to teammates and contributors on **Base**, with an on-chain record of every payment. Built on Scaffold-ETH 2 (Hardhat + Next.js).\n\n## What it does\n\n- Connect a wallet on Base.\n- Approve USDC and send a payout to any address (or ENS name) with an optional memo.\n- Every payout is emitted as a `Paid` event and shown in a live \"Latest payouts\" feed.\n\nThe `Payouts` contract pulls USDC from the sender via `transferFrom` and forwards it straight to the recipient — it never custodies funds.\n\n## Packages\n\n- `packages/hardhat` — `Payouts.sol` contract and deploy scripts.\n- `packages/nextjs` — Next.js frontend (`/payouts` page).\n\n## Development\n\n```bash\nyarn install\nyarn chain      # local chain\nyarn deploy     # deploy contracts\nyarn start      # frontend at http://localhost:3000\n```\n\nDeploy to Base:\n\n```bash\nyarn deploy --network base\nyarn verify --network base\n```\n\n## Configuration\n\n- Target network and RPC/polling settings: `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n- For production, set `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID` in the hosting environment rather than relying on the shared defaults.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":403,"cache_read_input_tokens":73936,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":403},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c3931a04-18eb-4b4e-b035-9a275c621ebf","uuid":"47eb98f6-98f5-46dd-a32f-ec0ce3cfa5c8","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:45:49.849Z","request_id":"req_011CdM9BfP3EZDuNE9MEPJ1G"}

Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.","+- Connect a wallet on Base.","+- Approve USDC and send a payout to any address (or ENS name) with an optional memo.","+- Every payout is emitted as a `Paid` event and shown in a live \"Latest payouts\" feed."," ","-⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Hardhat, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.","+The `Payouts` contract pulls USDC from the sender via `transferFrom` and forwards it straight to the recipient — it never custodies funds."," ","-- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.","-- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.","-- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.","-- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.","-- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.","+## Packages"," ","-![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)","+- `packages/hardhat` — `Payouts.sol` contract and deploy scripts.","+- `packages/nextjs` — Next.js frontend (`/payouts` page)."," ","-## Requirements","+## Development"," ","-Before you begin, you need to install the following tools:","-","-- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)","-- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))","-- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)","-","-## Quickstart","-","-To get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:","-","-1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:","-","-```","-cd my-dapp-example","+```bash"," yarn install","+yarn chain      # local chain","+yarn deploy     # deploy contracts","+yarn start      # frontend at http://localhost:3000"," ```"," ","-2. Run a local network in the first terminal:","+Deploy to Base:"," ","+```bash","+yarn deploy --network base","+yarn verify --network base"," ```","-yarn chain","-```"," ","-This command starts a local Ethereum network using Hardhat. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/hardhat/hardhat.config.ts`.","+## Configuration"," ","-3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:","-","-```","-yarn deploy","-```","-","-This command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/hardhat/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/hardhat/deploy` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.","-","-4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:","-","-```","-yarn start","-```","-","-Visit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.","-","-Run smart contract test with `yarn hardhat:test`","-","-- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/hardhat/contracts`","-- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.","-- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/hardhat/deploy`","-","-","-## Documentation","-","-Visit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.","-","-To know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).","-","-## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2","-","-We welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!","-","-Please see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","\\ No newline at end of file","+- Target network and RPC/polling settings: `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.","+- For production, set `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID` in the hosting environment rather than relying on the shared defaults."]}],"originalFile":"# 🏗 Scaffold-ETH 2\n\n<h4 align=\"center\">\n  <a href=\"https://docs.scaffoldeth.io\">Documentation</a> |\n  <a href=\"https://scaffoldeth.io\">Website</a>\n</h4>\n\n🧪 An open-source, up-to-date toolkit for building decentralized applications (dapps) on the Ethereum blockchain. It's designed to make it easier for developers to create and deploy smart contracts and build user interfaces that interact with those contracts.\n\n> [!NOTE]\n> 🤖 Scaffold-ETH 2 is AI-ready! It has everything agents need to build on Ethereum. Check `.agents/`, `.claude/`, `.opencode` or `.cursor/` for more info.\n\n⚙️ Built using NextJS, RainbowKit, Hardhat, Wagmi, Viem, and Typescript.\n\n- ✅ **Contract Hot Reload**: Your frontend auto-adapts to your smart contract as you edit it.\n- 🪝 **[Custom hooks](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/hooks/)**: Collection of React hooks wrapper around [wagmi](https://wagmi.sh/) to simplify interactions with smart contracts with typescript autocompletion.\n- 🧱 [**Components**](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/components/): Collection of common web3 components to quickly build your frontend.\n- 🔥 **Burner Wallet & Local Faucet**: Quickly test your application with a burner wallet and local faucet.\n- 🔐 **Integration with Wallet Providers**: Connect to different wallet providers and interact with the Ethereum network.\n\n![Debug Contracts tab](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/assets/55535804/b237af0c-5027-4849-a5c1-2e31495cccb1)\n\n## Requirements\n\nBefore you begin, you need to install the following tools:\n\n- [Node (>= v20.18.3)](https://nodejs.org/en/download/)\n- Yarn ([v1](https://classic.yarnpkg.com/en/docs/install/) or [v2+](https://yarnpkg.com/getting-started/install))\n- [Git](https://git-scm.com/downloads)\n\n## Quickstart\n\nTo get started with Scaffold-ETH 2, follow the steps below:\n\n1. Install dependencies if it was skipped in CLI:\n\n```\ncd my-dapp-example\nyarn install\n```\n\n2. Run a local network in the first terminal:\n\n```\nyarn chain\n```\n\nThis command starts a local Ethereum network using Hardhat. The network runs on your local machine and can be used for testing and development. You can customize the network configuration in `packages/hardhat/hardhat.config.ts`.\n\n3. On a second terminal, deploy the test contract:\n\n```\nyarn deploy\n```\n\nThis command deploys a test smart contract to the local network. The contract is located in `packages/hardhat/contracts` and can be modified to suit your needs. The `yarn deploy` command uses the deploy script located in `packages/hardhat/deploy` to deploy the contract to the network. You can also customize the deploy script.\n\n4. On a third terminal, start your NextJS app:\n\n```\nyarn start\n```\n\nVisit your app on: `http://localhost:3000`. You can interact with your smart contract using the `Debug Contracts` page. You can tweak the app config in `packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`.\n\nRun smart contract test with `yarn hardhat:test`\n\n- Edit your smart contracts in `packages/hardhat/contracts`\n- Edit your frontend homepage at `packages/nextjs/app/page.tsx`. For guidance on [routing](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/defining-routes) and configuring [pages/layouts](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/routing/pages-and-layouts) checkout the Next.js documentation.\n- Edit your deployment scripts in `packages/hardhat/deploy`\n\n\n## Documentation\n\nVisit our [docs](https://docs.scaffoldeth.io) to learn how to start building with Scaffold-ETH 2.\n\nTo know more about its features, check out our [website](https://scaffoldeth.io).\n\n## Contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2\n\nWe welcome contributions to Scaffold-ETH 2!\n\nPlease see [CONTRIBUTING.MD](https://github.com/scaffold-eth/scaffold-eth-2/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md) for more information and guidelines for contributing to Scaffold-ETH 2.","userModified":false}}

BigInt(blockNumber) : undefined);\n      if (maxBlock) {\n        batchToBlock = batchEndBlock < maxBlock ? batchEndBlock : maxBlock;\n      }\n\n      const data = await getEvents(\n        {\n          address: deployedContractData?.address,\n          event,\n          fromBlock: pageParam,\n          toBlock: batchToBlock,\n          args: filters,\n        },\n        publicClient,\n        { blockData, transactionData, receiptData },\n      );\n\n      setLastFetchedBlock(batchToBlock || blockNumber || 0n);\n\n      return data;\n    },\n    enabled: enabled && isContractAddressAndClientReady && !isPollingActive, // Disable when polling starts\n    initialPageParam: fromBlockValue,\n    getNextPageParam: (lastPage, allPages, lastPageParam) => {","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c3931a04-18eb-4b4e-b035-9a275c621ebf","uuid":"f8f1085e-2e75-41f3-a560-c17a772465da","timestamp":"2026-07-24T15:46:23.354Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"=== find scaffold-ui package ===\n=== Address dts ===\ndir: \n(eval):3: no matches found: /dist/*.d.ts\n=== event data type ===\n  });\n  const [liveEvents, setLiveEvents] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [lastFetchedBlock, setLastFetchedBlock] = useState<bigint | null>(null);\n  const [isPollingActive, setIsPollingActive] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: blockNumber } = useBlockNumber({ watch: watch, chainId: selectedNetwork.id });\n\n  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });\n\n  const event =\n    deployedContractData &&\n    ((deployedContractData.abi as Abi).find(part => part.type === \"event\" && part.name === eventName) as AbiEvent);\n\n  const isContractAddressAndClientReady = Boolean(deployedContractData?.address) && Boolean(publicClient);\n\n  const fromBlockValue =\n    fromBlock !== undefined\n      ?
